**解析（Kimi）**：产品：遥控器；声音表述：无；其他：赞扬亚马逊售后换货。

### S1-017　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Excellent computer and great price. Was looking for pure power. Wrote an algorithm to monitor stocks on the NY Stock Exchange. Watching every tick to detect institutions buying or selling. Code worked great but old computer could not handle scrubbing over 1 billion ticks per day to parse.  So I got The Beast by Vision. When looking at Task Manager the Beast is like humming along smoothly very fast and scrubbing the data from exchange and getting information looking for within milliseconds. A power horse for the business - Only using as a server. Happy got it. Cheers :D
**译文（Kimi）**：出色的电脑，价格也好。我要的就是纯粹算力。我写了个算法监控纽交所股票，逐笔抓取行情、识别机构买卖。代码本身跑得漂亮，但旧电脑扛不住每天清洗 10 亿+ 条 tick 数据来做解析，于是入手了 Vision 的「The Beast」。看任务管理器，它平稳飞转，飞快清洗交易所数据，毫秒级找到我要的信息。干活的动力猛兽——我只拿它当服务器用。买得开心。干杯 :D
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：高性能台式电脑/服务器；声音表述：无；其他：强调算力与大数据清洗性能。

### S2-088　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Ok, I have to admit I'm pretty impressed with this little camera. At first I thought I had to use the Haven subscription, especially after reading the reviews here which said it is necessary. I was kind of bummed about that at first but it turns out this is really useful even without! Sure, being able to record videos and store them in the cloud require a subscription as well as many of the advanced features, but you can still "arm" the camera to snap a photo and alert your phone when it detects motion, which it does splendidly even without a sub. The way I currently use it, it alerts my phone and I can then go to the live view and see and even interact with who is there (talk feature is really cool!).<br />On top of that, I set ours up with Amazon's Alexa voice service and it is EXTREMELY COOL! I think the speaker seems a bit louder than an Echo Dot, which surprised me. I'm mounting ours by the front porch so we can see people come to the door and interact with them, but we can also use it to play music via Amazon's Alexa when just sitting on the porch drinking coffee. That's an amazing perk that I wasn't expecting before we got it!<br />I do wish you could combine it with device music groups in Alexa (so I could say, for example, play this song on the everywhere group and it will play inside on our Echo and out on the porch with the Ecobee), but sadly only echo devices can be grouped. That's OK, I knew that before getting this as other devices in our home can't be grouped either (Facebook Portal or Kindles, for example).<br />If you want to spring for the subscription service, there's a TON of goodies and advanced features beyond even the cloud storage things. But even without that, the live view and photo motion alerts combined with the voice assistant / Alexa integration makes it worth it even just for the hardware alone!
**译文（Kimi）**：说实话，这台小摄像头让我挺惊喜。起初我以为非订阅 Haven 不可——尤其看完这里的评论都说订阅是必须的，我当时挺沮丧，结果事实证明不订阅也真的好用！没错，录像存云端要订阅，不少进阶功能也是，但你仍然可以让相机「设防」，检测到移动就拍照并推送手机，这功能不订阅也做得漂亮。我现在的用法是：它推送到我手机，我再打开实时画面，看看来人是谁，还能隔空对话（对讲功能很酷！）。更妙的是，我还把它接上了亚马逊 Alexa 语音服务，体验极度惊艳！我觉得它的喇叭似乎比 Echo Dot 还响，挺意外。我把它装在前廊，能看谁来敲门并隔空交流；坐在廊下喝咖啡时，还能通过 Alexa 用它放音乐——这是入手前没想到的超值彩蛋！我倒是希望它能并进 Alexa 的设备音乐组（那样我就能说「这首歌全屋播放」，屋里 Echo 和廊下这台一起响），可惜只有 Echo 设备能成组。也罢，买前我就知道，我家其他设备（Facebook Portal、Kindle 之类）也进不了组。如果你愿意掏钱订阅，云端存储之外还有一大堆进阶功能。但即便不订阅，实时画面＋移动拍照推送＋语音助手/Alexa 集成，光是硬件本身就值回票价了！
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：家用安防摄像头；声音表述：内置喇叭音量大于 Echo Dot（正面细节）；其他：无订阅仍可用核心功能；Alexa 集成是亮点；不能并入音乐组。

### S3-074　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Love this so much! Great sound quality. I was very skeptical st first but so glad I purchased this! The sound quality is much better than I thought. Sounds like my normal radio sound quality.
**译文（Kimi）**：太爱了！音质很棒。起初我很怀疑，现在庆幸买了！音质比预想的好太多，听着就像我平时听收音机的那种音质。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：音箱/收音机类设备；声音表述：音质好、超出预期，类比普通收音机音质（正面）；其他：由怀疑转为惊喜。

### S1-054　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Burned out my car charger and gets so hot that I burned my fingers on it. This will work great unless you have a qualcom 2.0 quick charging phone or charger. My note 4 isn't damaged yet, but I've burned out 2 qualcom chargers on this. If I charge with a regular charger everything is perfect!<br /><br />should come with a warning though.
**译文（Kimi）**：它烧坏了我的车充，还烫到让我手指烫伤。除非你用支持高通 2.0 快充的手机或充电器，否则它工作得很好。我的 Note 4 还没坏，但已经用它烧掉了两个高通充电器。换普通充电器充，一切完美！不过真该附个警示。

## 第 28 批

### S3-066　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Pros: small footprint (perfect anywhere), easy set up, easy controls, decent price for a small sound bar, hdmi arc, voice is clear.<br /><br />Cons: subwoofer is not strong barely noticeable, no bass. The sound is not consistent, sometimes I have to turn it way up when I play a video then when I play a different video the sound comes blaring out<br /><br />The voice comes out clear. There is no depth in the bass. I turn the subwoofer all the way up and it makes no difference. I turn the volume all the way up and the sound is very flat. I bought this because I had and hdmi eArc connection hoping for better sound but it was ok. For the price I paid it does pretty good
**译文（Kimi）**：优点：占地小（放哪都合适）、好装、好操作、小条形音箱里价格公道、支持 HDMI ARC、人声清晰。缺点：低音炮没劲、几乎察觉不到、没有低频；音量不稳定——这个视频要开很大声，换个视频又轰得刺耳。人声倒是清楚。低频毫无深度，把低音炮开到最大也没用；把总音量开满，声音又非常平。我买它是因为有 HDMI eARC 接口，指望音质更好，结果只能说还行。就我付的价来说，表现算不错了。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：条形音箱（soundbar）+ 低音炮；声音表述：人声清晰（正面）；低音缺失/无深度、音量输出不一致（负面）；其他：功能够用、期望值管理。

### S2-024　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I have been building computer systems and troubleshooting for a long time.  I really enjoy taking parts in and out and programming systems to do what I want.  I remember looking at a commercial for the new tablets and saying to my family- &#34;What the heck would anyone want one of THOSE things for?&#34;  It was not long after they became bored with my insistence that tablets were a total waste that I had my chance to take my first tablet for a test run- the Acer Iconia A1-830.<br /><br />The Acer was a fantastic little first tablet.  It performed as well or better than a few Samsungs and older Apples that we had the chance to put it up against, and for the price, it was a great little tablet.  I found some use for it and found myself reaching for the tablet when I didn't want to do work at the computer.  It was more portable than a laptop and easier to do quick surfing and simple tasks on, and GAMING.<br /><br />I thought that all was well until I began to have a few issues with the Acer.  It was at that point that I saw a great price on the new Samsung Galaxy Tab S2.  I was hooked.<br /><br />WHAT IS THIS TABLET LIKE?<br />I plan to write this review to people who are not as interested in the detailed specs of this unit, but instead I will write this for an average user who might not be as technically astute.  There are quite a few well done reviews that have lots of specs and details that will satisfy those who really need that information to make decisions.<br /><br />SCREEN<br />One of the things that I did not like about the older Samsungs was the size.  I preferred the more squared screen of the Acer than the Samsungs I had worked on.  My daughter has one and although it seems nice, I just was not keen on the screen.  This model has exactly what I wanted to have.  The shape makes it easier to work on the web , use as an E reader, and play games.  I find it easier to read than more elongated screens.<br /><br />One of the things that I found to be frustrating is the slick surface of the screen.  For web surfing or book reading it is fine, but games can really get frustrating as I find the screen to be very sensitive and it is easy to mess up.<br /><br />As for the brightness, it is very bright.  I have it turned down by about 75% and it is plenty bright enough most of the time.  Also, the color contrast is breathtaking.  The screen is very sharp and the blacks are BLACK and the colors are very saturated.  This thing is eye candy squared!  It is hard to look at other screens when I am done using this.<br /><br />COLOR<br />This is not really gold in color.  I would say it is gold tinged platinum in color.  Do not expect a bold gold.<br /><br />SMOOTHNESS AND UNEVEN EDGES<br />There are a few things which bug me about this tablet, but not that much.  Getting a good case from Fintie took care of it.  The button on the bottom of the vertical positioned screen stands out from the unit.  That means if you are going to get a keyboard in the case, it could press on the button and cause problems.  The Fintie case is raised enough to protect the button.<br /><br />There is also the camera lens on the back that sticks out quite a bit.  Again, the case takes care of that problem.  The back itself might be plastic, but it feels like metal and looks like metal so it is not cheap looking.<br /><br />SPEED AND FUNCTION<br />WOW is this thing FAST!  I have to say that it really has blown me out of the water with the speed and smooth operation of everything I have tried to do.  It works smoothly, load up is fast.  I know part of that might be due to the inclusion of Lollipop but also the hardware and set up.  I have not found it to lag at all.  Great performance.  I also know that this model is scheduled for the Marshmallow upgrade sometime this year which could change performance to be even better.<br /><br />I have  32G microSD card in this right now but it is expandable to 128.<br /><br />BATTERY LIFE<br />I was very concerned about all of the complaints about battery life.  I was so concerned I almost didn't get this tablet.  I am SO glad I did, however. I keep location turned off and bluetooth as well unless I am using it.  The battery stuns me with how long it has lasted.  I loaded that baby up to 100% last night and this morning about 11 AM I began to work intensely on the tablet, trying to back up files from my Acer and transfer them over to the Samsung via bluetooth.  That means that the bluetooth was on all day, I was downloading and installing all day long.  I have to say that I am shocked.  It is about 12 hours later and there is still over 50% battery left.  I had to recharge my Acer once during all of this time.<br /><br />So, compared to what I am used to (which is about 2-4 hours of work on a tablet before recharging, including no location turned on) this tablet is dreamy for me.  The battery is way beyond what I am used to with the Acer, so I am very happy.<br /><br />THE BOTTOM LINE<br />I will update as I find things to tell you about, but I wanted to start off with the basics as that is what I was looking for when I was trying to decide if I should get this tablet or not.  I still do not find tablets to be useful for all of my work, but they are great for smaller web surfing, quick look ups, apps for certain things and of course games and skype.  As for the keyboard and typing, it is not really practical at least for me to use this for heavier loads of work- desktop or laptop are best suited for that.<br /><br />Is it worth the price?  At $300 it is a very powerful device with a screen that blows anything else I have seen out of the water.  The colors are a feast for the eyes, the clarity is crisp, and the smooth operation is delightful.  If you really enjoy having a tablet you can depend on, I would say that this fits the bill.<br /><br />Could you do with something less expensive?  Yes.  But you might have more frustration with that lower price tag.  The set up of this tablet is easy to work with, great operation and having two windows open at the same time is delightful.  I am very happy with this and despite my misgivings after reading so many reviews with disappointment in them I can say at this point, I am very very glad that I went ahead and got it.  It is a really great tablet.
**译文（Kimi）**：我装机组装、排障多年，就喜欢折腾硬件、写程序让系统按我的想法跑。还记得当年看到新平板的广告，我跟家人说：「谁会要那玩意儿啊？」没过多久，他们听腻了我「平板纯属浪费」的论调，我恰好有机会试用了第一台平板——Acer Iconia A1-830。那台 Acer 作为入门平板棒极了，性能不输甚至胜过我们手头的几台三星和旧款苹果，就价格来说相当值。我确实找到了它的用途：不想在电脑前干活时，就顺手抄起平板。它比笔记本便携，随手刷刷网页、办点小事、打打游戏都方便。后来 Acer 出了点毛病，正好看到新款三星 Galaxy Tab S2 促销，我就上钩了。这台平板什么样？这篇评论写给对详细参数不感兴趣、技术不太精通的一般用户；想看参数和细节的话，这里不乏写得很好的评测。屏幕：老款三星我不满意的一点就是屏幕比例。比起我用过的三星，我更喜欢 Acer 那种偏方正的屏。我女儿有一台三星，看着不错，但我就是对那屏幕提不起劲。这款正好给了我要的：这个比例上网、当电子书、玩游戏都更顺手，比狭长屏好读。烦人的一点是屏幕表面太滑——刷网页、看书没事，打游戏就很抓狂，屏幕太灵敏，容易误触。亮度方面非常亮，我调到约 75% 以下，大多数时候都绰绰有余。色彩对比度令人惊叹：屏很锐，黑是真黑，颜色很饱和，这玩意儿就是「养眼花屏」的平方！用完它再看别的屏幕都看不下去。颜色：其实不是金色，要我说更像带金调的铂金色。别指望张扬的金色。顺滑度与边缘不平整：有几个小地方让我不爽，但问题不大，配个 Fintie 的好壳就解决了。竖屏时底部的按键凸出机身，如果配键盘壳，可能压到按键造成误触，Fintie 壳的隆起足够护住按键。背面摄像头镜片也凸出不少，同样靠壳解决。背板或许是塑料，但手感和观感都像金属，不显廉价。速度与功能：哇，这机器真快！我试过的所有操作都又快又顺，真的把我惊到了。运行顺滑、加载飞快——我知道部分得归功于 Lollipop 系统，但也有硬件和调教的功劳。完全没发现卡顿，性能出色。而且这款今年内计划升级 Marshmallow，性能可能更上一层楼。我现在插了一张 32G microSD 卡，最高可扩展到 128G。续航：看到那么多吐槽续航的评论，我当时很担心，担心到差点没买。现在庆幸买了。我平时关掉定位，蓝牙不用也关着。这电池的持久把我惊到了：昨晚充到 100%，今早 11 点左右我开始高强度使用——从 Acer 备份文件经蓝牙传到这台三星，也就是说蓝牙开了一整天，我整天都在下载安装。结果 12 小时后还剩 50% 以上电量，这期间 Acer 都充过一次电。跟我习惯的水平（平板干活 2–4 小时就得充）比，这台对我来说像做梦一样，续航远超 Acer，我非常满意。结论：有新发现我会再更新，但想先把基础体验讲清楚——当初我决定买不买时找的就是这类信息。我仍不认为平板能干我所有的工作，但刷刷网页、快速查资料、用些特定应用、当然还有打游戏和 Skype，都很棒。至于打字干重活，至少对我来说不现实——那种活还是台式机或笔记本合适。值这个价吗？300 美元买到一台性能强劲的设备，屏幕把我见过的其他产品都秒杀了。色彩赏心悦目、显示锐利、运行流畅讨喜。如果你真想要一台靠得住的平板，我说它正合适。能不能买更便宜的？能。但那个价位可能带来更多糟心。这台平板设置顺手、运行出色，还能双窗口同时开，非常惬意。总的我很满意——尽管看了那么多失望的评价心有疑虑，但此刻我想说：真的真的庆幸买了它。这是一台非常棒的平板。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：三星 Galaxy Tab S2 平板；声音表述：无；其他：超长续航、屏幕比例与显示素质是核心卖点；按键/镜头凸出需配壳；写作对象明确为普通用户。

### S3-038　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Owned these for a few months. Used two times because I wanted to give them another chance. So disappointed.  There is NO extra bass. There isn't hardly any at all. Missed the return window i think.
**译文（Kimi）**：买了几个月，只用过两次——因为我想再给它们一次机会。太失望了。根本没有额外的低音，几乎一点低频都没有。大概也过了退货期了。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：耳机/音箱（强调低频的产品）；声音表述：低音完全缺失（强烈负面，针对「重低音」卖点）；其他：错过退货窗口。

### S1-011　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I was pleasantly surprised by the selection of shows, movies, and music...although I was just notified that the Youtube app would not be available anymore :-(  I bought this device with a gift card. Best $35 spent. Cut my Uverse account and saved a bunch of money since I don't watch a lot of tv. And I can get Starz if I want it. The only thing I don't care for is the simplicity of the remote. I wish it was a little more like a TV remote.
**译文（Kimi）**：节目、电影和音乐的选择让我惊喜……不过刚收到通知说 YouTube 应用以后不能用了 :-( 这台设备是拿礼品卡买的。35 美元花得最值的一笔。退掉了 Uverse 账户，省了一大笔钱——反正我也不怎么看电视。想看 Starz 也能订。唯一不满意的是遥控器太简洁，希望更像电视遥控器一点。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：流媒体电视棒；声音表述：无；其他：内容生态与省钱是卖点；YouTube 下架是遗憾；遥控器设计吐槽。

### S3-062　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I go through a lot of USB cables for my multiple devices and thus far these have been the best deal.  These held up very well while traveling and just local use.  They charge my devices just as fast as the Samsung factory cable and are much more durable. This 4 pack is a great deal because it always handy to have the correct length handy.  In my opinion you can't go wrong here.
**译文（Kimi）**：我设备多用线费，到目前为止这套是最划算的。旅行和日常使用都很抗造。充电速度和三星原厂线一样快，耐用性还好得多。四条装很值，各种长度随手就有。要我说，买它不会错。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：USB 充电线（四条装）；声音表述：无；其他：充电速度对标原厂、耐用。

### S1-089　**判定**：_0

### S1-029　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I bought this simply for the big green numbers.  While I appreciate that an alarm is useful, I don't use it on this item.  Also, I do not and never intended to use the radio.  I never tried it and could not imagine that it would be satisfactory, but couldn't care less.  It may be better if they simply didn't include it, but I doubt that it adds much to the cost or size.  There are other large digit clocks but not many that are green.  I have a red one of another make that is significantly brighter, and I believe the green and blue diodes typically have less output than red ones.  These type of clock displays are not ideal for bright rooms and probably useless in sunlight.  But they work fine in a darker room like a bedroom.  Some people may even find the brightness too high for sleeping hours but it met all my own criteria.
**译文（Kimi）**：我买它纯粹是为了大号绿色数字。闹钟功能虽好，但我从不用它。收音机我也不用、本来也没打算用——从没试过，也想不出它能好到哪去，但完全无所谓。厂商干脆别装收音机可能更好，不过我怀疑它对成本和体积影响不大。大数字时钟别家也有，但绿色的不多。我另有一台别家的红色款，明显更亮；据我所知绿、蓝二极管的输出通常就是不如红色。这类时钟显示屏在亮房间里不理想，阳光下基本没法看；但在卧室这类暗环境里完全没问题。有人可能甚至觉得睡觉时太亮，但它满足了我全部的要求。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：带收音机的大数字电子钟；声音表述：未使用收音机且预期不高（中性提及「想不出它能好到哪去」）；其他：购买动机是绿色大数字显示；亮度环境适配性讨论。

### S2-040　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I lived and breathed full-sized Kensington trackball mice from about 1987 until my last one stopped functioning and I couldn't find them anymore...probably in the late '90s. Tried some others; all had small, lightweight balls that gave none of the feel and control of Kensinton's...Expert Mouse? Was that what it was called back then too? Because it was the same general design as the current Expert Mouse, but the body was computer-case white.<br /><br />Anyway, I had several of those (the quality going downhill with each iteration, as I recall) and loved them, and since they disappeared (as far as I could tell), I adapted, first to trackpads, which I've never liked much, and then back to traditional mice which don't give nearly the control of a trackball. Now, at last, it seems Kensington has again begun to produce truly worthwhile trackball mice, and this is one. Oh, it's _so_ good to have one of these again.<br /><br />I'm doing that thing I hate when other people do it and reviewing the product as soon as I've gotten it up and running, without any kind of experiential history behind my assessment. I'll come back and update this in the future if necessary. But so far: I'm happy, happy, happy to have a real, full-sized Kensington trackball mouse again, with a heavy, substantial ball. There are only two things about this device that I'm not crazy about: In my old Kensington mice, the ball rested on physical rollers and I could give the ball a good spin and it would keep spinning for a second before stopping; it spun much more freely in its cavity than this ball does (so far...maybe that's something that will break in?) [Update: yes, after about three days of use the ball rolls more smoothly and silently than the old one on rollers ever did]. And the other thing is the scrolling mechanism. It's weird. Despite the KensingtonWorks software trying to make us believe otherwise, the "scroll ring" itself actually has nothing to do with scrolling, nor with anything else - the "scroll ring" is not even a control, but just a static piece of chrome trim. What makes the mouse scroll is twisting the ball this way or that from the top, as if it were a knob. At first I thought the "scroll ring" wasn't working because I was trying to use it without touching the ball...which does nothing. And now that I've figured out how scrolling works (you rest your finger on the "scroll ring" and push and pull the side of the ball)...I'm not crazy about it. I'm getting used to it, but it's just a weird way to scroll (I can't for the life of me remember how I used to do it on the Expert Mouse decades ago). It does work though.<br /><br />(Update on the scrolling: I'm also not crazy about the way the KensingtonWorks software handles the scrolling speed adjustment. When you make the base pointer movement speed faster, the pointer continues to move smoothly, but just faster. When you make the scrolling speed faster though, you lose all smoothness - the software makes the scrolling faster by breaking it into successively larger and more jarring jumps. At the slowest speed, it's pretty smooth; at the highest speeds it's no longer really scrolling, but simply jumping abruptly from one screenful (or page) to the next. Increasing the scrolling speed just increases the size of these jumps, but I find them jarring and annoying starting at the second or third lowest speed setting.)<br /><br />I also had to figure out how to program one of the buttons as a click lock, since Kensington doesn't call it that anymore, but calls it "left drag", "right drag", and "middle drag" (and I have no idea what the difference is between those three - I just chose "left drag" and it's working fine).<br /><br />So far, those are my only gripes, all minor. So happy to have one of these things again!  =) (Did I mention that?)<br /><br />Enjoy...
**译文（Kimi）**：大约从 1987 年起，全尺寸 Kensington 轨迹球鼠标曾是我的命根子，直到最后一只报废、再也买不到为止——大概是 90 年代末。其间试过别的品牌：全都是小而轻的球，完全没有 Kensington 的手感和操控……Expert Mouse？当年也叫这个名吗？反正和现在的 Expert Mouse 是同一通用设计，只是机身是机箱白。我先后拥有过好几只（印象中每换一代质量就下滑一点），爱得不行。它们消失之后，我先改用触控板——我从来不太喜欢——后来又退回传统鼠标，操控性远不如轨迹球。现在 Kensington 似乎终于又开始做真正值得买的轨迹球鼠标了，这只是其中之一。啊，重新拥有它的感觉实在太好了！我正在做我最讨厌别人做的事——刚装好就写评论，毫无使用历史背书。有必要的话以后再来更新。但到目前为止：能重新拥有一只真正的全尺寸 Kensington 轨迹球、配一颗沉甸甸的球，我开心、开心、开心。这设备只有两点让我不满意：一是老 Kensington 的球架在物理滚轴上，我可以猛拨一下让它自转一秒才停，在腔体里转得比现在这颗自由得多（目前如此……也许会越用越顺？【更新：会的，用了三天后，滚球比老滚轴款转得更顺、更安静】）。二是滚动机制，很怪。KensingtonWorks 软件想让我们信以为真，但「滚动环」本身与滚动毫无关系——它甚至不是个控件，只是一圈静态镀铬装饰。真正负责滚动的是从顶部横向扭动滚球，像拧旋钮一样。起初我以为「滚动环」坏了，因为我不碰球去转它——毫无反应。等搞懂滚动原理（手指搭在「滚动环」上，推拉球的侧面）……我还是不太喜欢。正在适应中，但这滚动方式实在怪（几十年前在 Expert Mouse 上怎么滚的，我怎么也想不起来了）。好歹能用。（滚动更新：我也不喜欢 KensingtonWorks 调滚动速度的方式。调指针速度时，指针依然平滑，只是变快；调滚动速度时却完全失去平滑——软件是靠把滚动切成越来越大、越来越突兀的跳变来「加速」的。最低速还算平滑；最高速下根本不算滚动，而是整屏/整页地硬跳。提高滚动速度只是放大跳变的幅度，从第二、三档开始我就觉得突兀烦人。）我还得琢磨怎么把某个键设成点击锁定——Kensington 如今不叫这个名了，改叫「左拖」「右拖」「中拖」（这三个有什么区别我完全不知道，我选了「左拖」，工作正常）。到目前为止，我的牢骚就这些，全是小问题。能重新拥有这玩意儿太开心了！=)（我提过了吗？）祝用得愉快……
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：Kensington 全尺寸轨迹球鼠标；声音表述：无；其他：复古情怀驱动的长评；滚球顺滑度经磨合改善；滚动机制与软件调速是主要槽点；含两次使用更新。

### S3-087　**判定**：_1
**原文**：Budget-priced earbuds are complicated to review because, in my experience, all $200 earbuds sound significantly better than all $50 earbuds, but there is still a lot of demand for value-priced "good enough" products. I do own a couple of $200 earbuds and they do sound noticeably richer and clearer than these $40 earbuds. However, when I'm out jogging or traveling (eg airplane or train) or listening to voice podcasts, having the absolute best sound quality isn't really important to me. In those situations, features are more important to me, like active noise cancellation, equalization, good volume, good battery life, and easy access to the features. I'd rather use cheap earbuds that I don't mind losing somewhere along the way.<br /><br />These earbuds have average quality sound for the $40 price. Where these Donner earbuds beat the competition is the features. I've never owned $40 earbuds with app control before. The user manual has a QR code to let you download the app. The QR code did not work, but I did find the "Donner Control" app in the Google Play app store and the app works fine on my Android phone. The app lets me pick from pre-programmed equalization settings or design my own. Adjusting the equalization for different types of music makes a significant difference in audio clarity. The app also lets me choose from 3 active noise cancellation modes. These features don't work as well as in $200 earbuds, but these are $40 earbuds and most $40 earbuds don't have any of these features or any app at all. You can control some of these features with the confusing set of triple-tap commands on the earbuds, but I can never remember those and would rather just tap the picture in the app.<br /><br />The app also lets you download firmware updates from Donner. None was available when I checked, but this could be a great way for them to refine the features of the earbuds or even add new features. They ask you to give them your email address and create an account when you open the app, but I did not and the core features of the app still work OK.<br /><br />Battery life for these earbuds is good for this price range (6 to 8 hours, depending on whether or not you use ANC). As with most budget-priced wireless earbuds, maximum volume is OK but not great. Bass is above average for the $40 price. Static noise is very low. The Bluetooth connection has been very stable for me, even when I'm 30 feet away from my phone or in a different room in the house.<br /><br />I can't give these earbuds 5 stars because I can easily buy better sound quality with more money, but these are solid 4 stars for value at the current $40 price.
**译文（Kimi）**：平价耳机很难评——以我的经验，所有 200 美元档耳机都比所有 50 美元档明显好听，但「够用就好」的平价产品依然需求巨大。我确实有几副 200 美元档耳机，它们听起来确实比这副 40 美元的更丰润、更清晰。不过跑步、出行（飞机、火车）或听语音播客时，极致音质对我并不重要。那些场景下我更看重功能：主动降噪、均衡调节、足够的音量、不错的续航，以及功能调用方便。我宁可戴丢了也不心疼的便宜耳机。就 40 美元价位而言，这副耳机的音质是中等水平。Donner 这副赢竞品赢在功能上。我从没拥有过带 App 控制的 40 美元耳机。说明书里有二维码可下载 App，二维码扫不出来，但我在 Google Play 找到了「Donner Control」，在我安卓手机上运行正常。App 里可以选预设均衡或自定义，针对不同音乐类型调均衡能明显改善声音清晰度。App 还提供 3 档主动降噪可选。这些功能的效果不如 200 美元档耳机，但这是 40 美元耳机，同价位大多数产品既没有这些功能也没有 App。部分功能也能用耳机上的三击指令控制，但那套指令让人糊涂，我永远记不住，宁可直接点 App 里的图标。App 还能下载 Donner 的固件更新，我查的时候没有可用版本，但这可能是厂商打磨乃至新增功能的好途径。打开 App 时它要求填邮箱注册账户，我没注册，核心功能照样正常用。这个价位的续航算不错（开不开降噪 6 到 8 小时）。跟多数平价真无线一样，最大音量尚可、不出彩。低音高于 40 美元档平均水平。底噪非常低。蓝牙连接非常稳，离手机 30 英尺或在另一个房间都不掉。我给它打不了 5 星——多花点钱就能轻松买到更好音质——但按 40 美元的现价，扎实值 4 星。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：Donner 平价真无线耳机；声音表述：音质中等（价位平均）、低音高于同价平均、底噪低、最大音量一般、调均衡明显改善清晰度（中性，按价位定位）；其他：App 控制与三档降噪是差异化卖点；蓝牙稳定；四星性价比结论。

### S1-076　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Lightning cables are something that we all need in the house. I selected this one with the thought of having it on the main level of the house, to be available to all of us when we want to charge our phones without hunting down a cable. So far it has worked out very well for us and is one of the better cables with which I have worked.<br /><br />This is a flat cable as opposed to round and I've found this has a couple of big advantages. First, it's a lot less likely get get tangled or kinked which is great. It can be tangled, of course, but it is a lot less likely. The other big plus is that flat cables seem a lot less likely to break near the connectors for some reason. As such, I expect this cable to last for a while and to be easily accessible as long as it's not tangled up with other cables.<br /><br />In use it works great. It charges my phone without issue and it also works just fine as a data cable. I did not see any mention of being it MFi certified anywhere, but it seems to be just fine regardless.<br /><br />So far I have no complaints with this cable. It works, and I like the design.
**译文（Kimi）**：Lightning 线是家里人人都需要的。我选这条是想放在一楼公共区域，谁想充电都不用到处找线。到目前为止它表现得非常好，是我用过比较好的线之一。这是扁线而非圆线，我发现这有两点大好处：第一，大大不容易缠结打弯——当然也能缠，只是概率低得多；第二，扁线在接头附近 somehow 就是更不容易断。所以我预计这条线能用挺久，只要别和其他线缠在一起，随手就能拿到。实际使用很棒：给手机充电没问题，当数据线用也完全正常。我没在任何地方看到它标 MFi 认证，但用起来似乎毫无影响。目前我对这条线零抱怨。能用，而且我喜欢这设计。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：Lightning 数据线（扁线）；声音表述：无；其他：扁线抗缠绕、接头处耐用；未提及 MFi 认证但使用正常。

### S2-001　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I got this really small portable DVD player.  That way if we lose the internet again (like we have so much this year already for days at a time) I can put in a DVD of some of my art classes and carry it on site where I want to paint or draw my lessons.  The problem is it had no case.<br /><br />HOW THIS COMES<br />This comes in a plastic cellophane sleeve.<br /><br />STRAP<br />There is a strap inside with a little padding on it for the shoulder.  It is adjustable.<br /><br />HANDLES<br />The handles are easy to carry if you don't want to use the strap.<br /><br />POCKET<br />There is a zipper pocket on the front which is perfect for the charging plug and cord.<br /><br />INSIDE<br />The inside is padded and larger enough to include the plastic folder where the DVD is so that I can easily carry both to my location and they are protected.<br /><br />DESIGN<br />The pencil design is bright, colorful, and not overly blurry so it looks pretty and not muddy.<br /><br />THE BOTTOM LINE<br />This is the perfect case to help protect my portable rechargeable DVD player when I want to use art classes in my shed or when we lose the internet.<br /><br />MY RATING<br />FIVE STARS.  Although this is not hard and does not protect a lot, it is perfect for what I need at this time for my player and DVD cases.
**译文（Kimi）**：我有一台很小的便携式 DVD 播放机。这样万一再断网（今年已经有好几次一断就是好几天），我可以放进艺术课的 DVD，带到我想画画写生的地方跟着学。问题是它没有包。包装：寄来只套了一个玻璃纸袋。背带：内含一条肩带，肩部带一点垫衬，长度可调。提手：不想用背带时，提手拎着很方便。口袋：正面有个拉链袋，放充电头和线正合适。内部：内衬有填充，空间够大，连装 DVD 的塑料盒也能一并放进去，两样都带着出门也有保护。设计：铅笔图案鲜亮多彩、不糊，看着漂亮不脏。结论：这就是完美适配我需求的包——在工棚里上艺术课或断网时保护我的便携式充电 DVD 机。我的评分：五星。虽说它不是硬壳、保护有限，但对目前我和机器、碟盒的需求来说刚刚好。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：便携 DVD 播放机保护包；声音表述：无；其他：分节评测结构；五星但坦承保护有限、贵在适配需求。

### S3-079　**判定**：_0
**原文**：None of these cheapo cameras have audio thats any good. Anything at high volume is distorted. I wish they would put a compressor mic of some kind on these. Picture okay but audio stunk..returned
**译文（Kimi）**：这类廉价摄像机的音频就没一个好的。音量一大就失真。真希望它们能装个带压缩的麦克风。画面还行，但音频太臭……退货了。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：廉价摄像机；声音表述：音频差、高音量失真，希望加压缩麦克风（负面）；其他：画面尚可但音频导致退货。

### S3-015　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Don't know if the unit I received is defective or if the specifications in the product description are just wrong, but this device is not working for me. The product description says it supports USB-C PD (Power Delivery) fast charging, which is the modern standard for fast charging that is supported by Apple, Google, and other. My phone supports Power Delivery and works great with all other Power Delivery chargers that I have tried. This device does not activate the Power Delivery charging mode on my phone and the charging speeds I get are dismal. Curiously, the product manual never mentions Power Delivery even though this is prominently listed in the Amazon product description. Also, this device is not UL Listed, which would indicate independent electrical safety testing. Doesn't work plus no independent certification = 1 star from me.
**译文（Kimi）**：不知道是我这台坏了，还是商品描述里的规格压根就是错的——反正这设备对我不起作用。描述说它支持 USB-C PD（Power Delivery）快充，这是苹果、谷歌等都支持的现代快充标准。我的手机支持 PD，和其他所有 PD 充电器配合都很好。可这设备激活不了手机的 PD 充电模式，充电速度慢得可怜。蹊跷的是，产品手册自始至终没提 PD，尽管亚马逊描述里把它当卖点醒目标注。另外，这设备没有 UL 认证——那代表经过独立电气安全测试。不能用＋没有独立认证＝我给 1 星。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：USB-C 充电器/移动电源；声音表述：无；其他：PD 快充疑似虚标、手册与描述矛盾、无 UL 认证，一星差评。

### S1-032　**判定**：_0
**原文**：Keep your desktop off the ground or even your gaming console. This will work great for either and keep them off the floor, reducing the amount of dust that can get into your system. It is very easy to put together and comes with everything you need except the tools. I will be assembling mine shortly and moving things around on my desk.

### S3-088　**判定**：_1
**原文**：Okay, For a pair of headphones that cost me less than 10 bucks, I didn't expect anything that great anyways. You get what you pay for, they're not bad, but they're not that great either. This is why: Sound quality is pretty weak, it's not just flat, but a bit tinny, on the trebles and the bass is pretty weak. The fit is unergonomic, it's just this generic round shape that runs a little big, so if you have small ears, the fit will be awkward. I've only had it for a week so, so far there are no problems with the durability. I like how the plug end is flexible, so I know int he future the wires aren't gonna fray and break. The cable is pretty flexible too, I don;t anticipate any crimping. The plastic housing on the wires and ear pieces themselves are alright for the price. I bought these cause I wanted some cheap disposable in ears for cycling. I can still hear what's going on outside while wearing these. They're a bit quieter than my regular in ear headphones. The bottom line is, if you want cheap earphones that work decently, these are fine. If you're used to better sound quality, these may jar you.<br />I also want to compare these to the earbuds that came with my zune player. The Zune earbuds are much better in sound quality and fit. After using it for two years, they broke so I bought these as a replacement. For around the same price, under 10 dollars, the zune ones are better.
**译文（Kimi）**：好吧，不到 10 美元的耳机，我本来也没指望多出色。一分钱一分货：不算差，但也好不到哪去。原因如下：音质相当弱——不只是平，还有点单薄发飘，高音如此，低音也弱。佩戴不符合人体工学，就是个通用的圆形状，而且偏大——耳朵小的人戴着会别扭。只用了一周，耐用性目前没问题。我喜欢插头那段是软的，将来线不至于磨损断裂。线材也挺软，预计不会折损。线材和耳机单元的塑料外壳就价格来说还行。买它是因为我想要一副骑车用的便宜「一次性」入耳耳机。戴着它仍能听到外界动静。音量比我常用的入耳耳机小一些。底线是：想要一副能正常干活的便宜耳塞，它没问题；但如果你习惯更好的音质，它可能让你难受。我还想拿它跟 Zune 播放器附赠的耳机比一比：Zune 那副音质和佩戴都好得多。用了两年后它坏了，我才买这副当替代品。价位相当、都不到 10 美元，Zune 那副更好。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：廉价有线入耳耳机；声音表述：音质弱、单薄发飘、高低音均弱、音量偏小（负面但有价格预期管理）；其他：佩戴不贴合；插头软线防断是加分项；骑行场景要能听到环境声故音量小可接受；与 Zune 附赠耳机对比落了下风。

### S3-073　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I bought 3 different pic printers to compare. The Kodak mini 2, poloroid mint and poloroid zip. Poloroid mint has the worst picture by miles compared to the other two. Very dark and grainy. I printed the same pic and it was horrible. It also froze the first two times i tried to print, so I had to take the blue alignment paper out otherwise it wouldn't work, unlike the other poloroud printer. The Kodak mini 2 has superior quality, and the poloroid zip comes in second. Zip is darker and more grainy. The kodak mini 2 has the brightest most color matching and you can make out details. Best picture. Returning the other two.  Only giving it two stars because I got it to print. Thats it.
**译文（Kimi）**：我买了三台照片打印机对比：柯达 Mini 2、宝丽来 Mint 和宝丽来 Zip。宝丽来 Mint 的画质比另外两台差出一大截——又暗又糙。同一张照片打出来惨不忍睹。头两次打印还卡死，非得取出蓝色校准纸才能工作，另一台宝丽来就没这毛病。柯达 Mini 2 画质最好，宝丽来 Zip 排第二——Zip 偏暗偏糙。柯达 Mini 2 最亮、色彩最准、细节可辨，画质最佳。另外两台退了。给这台两星纯粹是因为好歹能打印出来。就这样。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：便携照片打印机（三台对比横评）；声音表述：无；其他：横评结构；Mint 被批画质最差、还卡死；两星只因「能打印」。

### S1-052　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I only use this on demand, so don’t know how motion etc works. But when I use it the picture is great with good app response.  Very small and discrete.
**译文（Kimi）**：我只在需要时用，所以移动侦测之类的功能效果如何不清楚。但用起来画面很棒，App 响应也快。机身很小巧、不显眼。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：家用摄像头；声音表述：无；其他：按需使用、画质与 App 体验好、体积小巧。

**译文（Kimi）**：太爱这个语音识别了……我们家现在有两台。丈夫把那台旧的放在「男人窝」看 NBA 联盟通……我用楼上那台看亚马逊 Prime 视频和电视节目！以前我都在 Fire 或平板上看的。现在我女儿也想要一台了。

### S1-004　**判定**：_0
**原文**：i purchased this stand to free up some real estate on my desk. it took me about 30 min to have the stand assembled, i am certain it could be assembled faster, but i am just not so handy. the product is durable, its holding two of HP - 27w with ease. the second reason for me to go for this product versus many others was, you can swivel the monitors. you can align them on top of eachother, so if i need to add additional two monitors to my desk, i can purchase an another one of these mounts, and i wont be able to sacrificing any of my desk space
**译文（Kimi）**：买这个支架是为了腾出桌面空间。组装花了约 30 分钟——肯定能更快，只是我手不太巧。产品很结实，轻轻松松架着两台 HP 27w。选它而不选其他的第二个原因是显示器可以旋转：两台可以上下叠放对齐，以后桌面要再加两台显示器，我可以再买一支这种支架，一点桌面空间都不用牺牲。
**解析（Kimi）**：产品：双显示器支架；声音表述：无；其他：结实、可旋转、可堆叠扩展。

### S2-012　**判定**：_0
**原文**：I am blown away at the sound coming from this tiny inexpensive speaker. It rivals my JBL dock and my Bose dock. I am a stickler for sound quality & you really have to depend on the reviews for these items. You won’t be disappointed.
**译文（Kimi）**：这小不点廉价音箱发出的声音让我惊呆了。足以媲美我的 JBL 底座音箱和 Bose 底座音箱。我对音质很挑剔，这类东西真得靠评论来挑。你不会失望的。
